Smoke only: False
Snapshots 69/72; audit rows 1260/1296; initial probes 280/288.
Total NFE = solver evaluations per step times integration steps, plus endpoint denoising; cancellation uses the same solver at the finer reference resolution.
Relative FD compares each post-trained model with its matched Base sampler: ratio=post/Base; gain=1-ratio. Base FD must be finite and strictly positive; invalid inputs raise an error.
The FD ratio cancels a common uniform rescaling of feature coordinates, apart from numerical tolerances; it is not invariant to arbitrary changes of encoder or feature geometry.
Corrected_W2_squared_Rd uses exact empirical uniform squared-cost assignments; Corrected_Sinkhorn_divergence_Rd uses regularized OT with canceling same-cloud self terms. Both use independent-cloud correction at c=1 and retain signed squared-cost values without a square root or clipping.
Base/DAE training forwards use native CUDA BF16 when supported; parameters/losses, reference-teacher training, and post-training/evaluation forwards stay FP32. FD moments and Bures algebra are FP64.
Sampler activations are checkpointed with use_reentrant=False only while gradients are enabled.

Two larger real-only networks have independent initializations, training data and noise. They are shared across schedules.
References are selected using separate real validation data. Audit data are not used for selection.
Agreement between references is necessary context, not a guarantee: both can share systematic bias.
Real denoising MSE includes irreducible variance. Lower real MSE does not prove accuracy on generated inputs.
Repeated real-audit rows reuse identical inputs and are not independent trials.
legacy-time-base: uniform t on the previous time interval, with the frozen Base teacher.
matched-noise-reference: identical log-uniform sigma distribution and range across schedules, with the learned real-data reference ensemble. Both conditions use one fake update per G update.
Matching physical noise deliberately changes the previous weighted objective. No importance correction restores uniform-time weighting.
The two conditions share initial fake state and optimizer, but differ in both teacher and noise sampling.
This remains a pseudo-Huber endpoint score-difference surrogate, not measured KL and not full TDM.
Base teachers and samplers still differ across schedules. Noise matching does not remove those differences.

Training time includes shared fake-score warm-up where applicable.
Initial A2/A3 probes use the common probe_batch.
Generator steps are matched; independent real-reference training costs extra.
Single Base training seed: no claim of statistical confirmation. CPU smoke results validate execution only.
Final model archives are disabled. Base/DAE and selected reference preparation can be reused by verified provenance; interrupted post-training is not resumed.
Preparation provenance and cache events are recorded in preparation-cache.json; historical cached training is not charged as current-run training.

Complete machine-readable settings are saved in run.json.

QUALITY METRIC DEFINITION
Corrected_W2_squared_Rd and Corrected_Sinkhorn_divergence_Rd use 0.5*(h(P1,Q1)+h(P2,Q2)-h(P1,P2)-h(Q1,Q2)), with two independent target clouds P1/P2 and two independent generated clouds Q1/Q2.
h is exact empirical assignment mean squared Euclidean cost for W2 squared, or regularized OT with epsilon=0.1 for Sinkhorn; Sinkhorn same-cloud self terms cancel.
c=1: samples are unscaled. Both corrected estimates are signed squared-cost values; negative values are retained, with no square root or clipping.
Production counts are 50000 points per independent cloud for each global Sinkhorn solve and 5000 per cloud for empirical W2 squared; clouds are not split in half.
Independent replica seed offset=1000003. Seeds and subset indices are shared across matched Base/post evaluations. Target-target scalar baselines are cached once per metric sampling configuration in a run.
Small shadow probes and smoke runs are separately marked by sample counts; they are not full-quality estimates.
GeomLoss online/KeOps uses linear storage, not linear pairwise compute; scaling=0.9 is a numerical approximation, not an exact OT certificate.
These corrected finite-sample statistics are not certificates of population W2 or metric validity; read frozen-feature FD and mode coverage alongside them.
training_surrogate is the optimized scalar, not measured population KL. Positive metric gain means Base minus post-trained.

GRADIENT FLOW AUDIT (before clipping)
Architecture: pre-normalized residual blocks, depth-scaled skips, LeakyReLU slope0.1, nonzero initialized output heads.
Monitoring is evidence, not a guarantee against vanishing gradients or score/feature underfitting.
Frozen teachers/encoders intentionally have no parameter updates; endpoint input gradients remain audited by A2/A3.
Small gradients can reflect convergence or exact zero score difference; flags alone do not establish vanishing gradients.
Detailed per-parameter pre-clip gradients: Gradient-health.jsonl.
